{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.claude/skills/standards\n\n# Ethereum Standards\n\nAll of these are deployed and in production use, not proposals: **ERC-8004** (agent identity + reputation, Ethereum mainnet 2026-01-29), **x402** (HTTP payments), **EIP-3009** (gasless USDC transfers, what makes x402 practical), **EIP-7702** (smart EOAs, live with Pectra 2025-05-07). When a design needs agent discovery, third-party-attested reputation, per-call billing without accounts, or contract abilities on an address a user already owns, name the standard instead of rebuilding it — an API-key tier, a reviews table you host, or a migration to a fresh smart account are all the wrong answer to a problem one of these already solves.\n\n## ERC-8004 — agent identity and reputation\n\nTwo registries, deployed at the same pair of addresses across 40+ chains — but **the testnet set is a different pair**, and the mainnet addresses have no code on Sepolia or Base Sepolia:\n\n| | mainnets | testnets |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| IdentityRegistry (ERC-721) | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` | `0x8004A818BFB912233c491871b3d84c89A494BD9e` |\n| ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` | `0x8004B663056A597Dffe9eCcC1965A193B7388713` |\n\n- `register()` mints an ERC-721. **The agentId is the tokenId the registry assigns** — never a UUID, hash, or slug you pick. Callers address the agent chain-scoped: `agentRegistry: eip155:8453:0x8004A169…` (Base) plus that `agentId`.\n- The `agentURI` (the ERC-721 tokenURI) MUST resolve to the registration file — `ipfs://`, `https://`, or a base64 `data:` URI. It carries `type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\"`, `name`, `description`, `image`, a `services` array (A2A card, MCP, ENS, DID, email…), `x402Support`, `active`, a `registrations` array of `{agentId, agentRegistry}`, and optional `supportedTrust`.\n- **Bind the endpoint domain, the step that gets skipped.** If the domain serving the advertised endpoints is not the domain serving the agentURI file, publish `https://{endpoint-domain}/.well-known/agent-registration.json` with at least a `registrations` entry whose `agentRegistry` and `agentId` match the onchain agent. An A2A `agent-card.json` is a different document and does not do this. If the same domain already serves the agentURI file, control is proven there — say which of the two you are relying on rather than leaving the endpoints unbound.\n- Reputation is **client-attested**: the caller who paid posts `giveFeedback(agentId, value, valueDecimals, tag1, tag2, endpoint, feedbackURI, feedbackHash)`; the registry rejects feedback from the agent's owner and operators, so you cannot rate yourself. Values are fixed point — 99.77% uptime is `value=9977, valueDecimals=2`; 87/100 quality is `value=87, valueDecimals=0`. Read with `getSummary(agentId, clientAddresses, tag1, tag2)` and filter to clients you trust; unfiltered totals are Sybil bait.\n- The spec also defines a Validation Registry (re-execution, zkML, TEE attestation, 0–100 validator scores). It is not part of the deployed pair above — check before designing on it.\n- The contracts are live; the EIP is still **Draft**, so treat anything you have not verified onchain as movable. Registries are ERC-1967 proxies: read the implementation slot if a signature surprises you.\n\n## x402 — pay per HTTP call, no accounts\n\n`GET` → `402` + `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` → client signs → retry with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` → server verifies and settles → `200` + `PAYMENT-RESPONSE`. A facilitator (Coinbase runs a public one, anyone can run their own) performs verify/settle so the resource server never runs a node or holds gas.\n\nAmounts go on the wire in **token base units**: $0.35 USDC is `350000` (USDC has 6 decimals) — not `0.35`, not an 18-decimal figure. Native USDC on Base is `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`; `0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48` is mainnet USDC and `0xd9aAEc86B65D86f6A7B5B1b0c42FFA531710b6CA` is the bridged USDbC — both are the wrong contract for a Base x402 route.\n\nThe maintained TypeScript line is the scoped one: `@x402/core`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/fetch`, `@x402/express`, with `@coinbase/x402` as the facilitator config beside it. Unscoped `x402`, `x402-fetch`, `x402-express` are frozen at 1.2.0; Go lives at `github.com/x402-foundation/x402/go/v2`. **Do not write the integration from a remembered snippet** — `x402Fetch` and `createWallet` do not exist in the scoped packages, and a one-argument `paymentMiddleware(config)` is the v1 shape. Read the installed types: on 2.x the Express middleware takes routes plus a resource server, and the fetch wrapper takes an x402 client, not a bare account.\n\n**A caller with USDC and no ETH can still pay**, because USDC implements **EIP-3009**: the client signs a `transferWithAuthorization` offchain and the server or facilitator submits it and pays the gas. That is the mechanism to name — not ERC-4337 bundlers, not an unnamed \"relayer\", not ERC-2612 permit followed by `transferFrom`.\n\n## EIP-7702 — contract code on an EOA the user already has\n\nLive on Ethereum mainnet since Pectra, **2025-05-07**. The EOA signs an authorization **with its own key** (a type `0x04` transaction) that writes a delegation designator, `0xef0100 || implementation address`, into the account. Calls to that same address then execute the implementation's code — an approve+swap batched atomically, sponsored gas, session-key auth — with the address, key, ENS name and history untouched. Deploying a fresh ERC-4337 wallet instead puts the user at a **new address**, which is what to reject whenever the existing address has to survive.\n\nThe delegation **persists**. It stays in place until a later authorization replaces it, or clears it by delegating to the zero address. It is not single-transaction-only and does not auto-revert when the batched call finishes.\n\n## Status at a glance\n\n| Standard | What | Status |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| ERC-8004 | Agent identity + reputation | Live — Ethereum mainnet 2026-01-29 (EIP still Draft) |\n| x402 | HTTP 402 payments | Live — scoped SDKs on 2.x |\n| EIP-3009 | Gasless token transfers | Live — USDC implements it |\n| EIP-7702 | Smart EOAs | Live — Pectra, 2025-05-07 |\n| ERC-4337 | Account abstraction (new account, new address) | Live, widely deployed |\n| ERC-2612 / ERC-4626 / ERC-6551 | Permit / tokenized vaults / token-bound accounts | Live |\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"c0c00b68-2b31-4b1a-8278-0d968dc3aad0","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:26:31.863Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PnQdYhzg83Gz7LWr5wiNX4","type":"tool_result","content":"     * @returns The attempt's result\n     * @throws FacilitatorTimeoutError when the deadline elapses before completion\n     */\n    private withRequestTimeout;\n    /**\n     * Helper to convert objects to JSON-safe format.\n     * Handles BigInt and other non-JSON types.\n     *\n     * @param obj - The object to convert\n     * @returns The JSON-safe representation of the object\n     */\n    private toJsonSafe;\n}\n\n/**\n * Which settle invocation is running for a payment.\n *\n * - `before-handler` — settle before the resource handler (e.g. escrow deposit)\n * - `after-handler` — settle after the resource handler (authorization charge, escrow charge)\n * - `cancel` — refund/close settle from verified-payment cancellation\n *\n * Settle lifecycle hooks (`beforeSettle`, `afterSettle`, `onSettleFailure`,\n * `enrichSettlementPayload`, `enrichSettlementResponse`) fire once per settle.\n * Multi-settle flows (`escrow`) therefore invoke them more than once; branch on\n * this field when a hook has side effects that must not double-run.\n */\ntype SettlePhase = \"before-handler\" | \"after-handler\" | \"cancel\";\n/**\n * Configuration for a protected resource\n * Only contains payment-specific configuration, not resource metadata\n */\ninterface ResourceConfig {\n    scheme: string;\n    /**\n     * Payment recipient. Use a **vacant** value (`\"\"` or whitespace-only) when an extension must\n     * fill `payTo` during `enrichPaymentRequiredResponse`; non-vacant values are **immutable** there\n     * so extensions cannot redirect funds to an arbitrary address.\n     */\n    payTo: string;\n    price: Price;\n    network: Network;\n    maxTimeoutSeconds?: number;\n    extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n/**\n * Context for `enrichPaymentRequiredResponse`. Extensions may merge extension payload via the\n * return value. In-place edits to `paymentRequiredResponse.accepts` are **allowlisted** only\n * (see {@link assertAcceptsAllowlistedAfterExtensionEnrich}): `scheme`, `network`, and\n * `maxTimeoutSeconds` are immutable; `payTo`, `amount`, and `asset` may change only when the\n * baseline value was vacant; `extra` may add keys but must not change or remove baseline keys.\n * `extra.paymentFlow` and `extra.assetTransferMethod` are protocol-reserved and immutable\n * during enrichment (enrichment must not add or rewrite them).\n */\ninterface PaymentRequiredContext {\n    requirements: PaymentRequirements[];\n    resourceInfo: ResourceInfo;\n    error?: string;\n    paymentRequiredResponse: PaymentRequired;\n    transportContext?: unknown;\n}\n/**\n * Verify / settle lifecycle hook context: treat as **read-only** for core protocol fields.\n * Control flow uses **abort** / **recover** return values only, not in-place mutation.\n */\ninterface VerifyContext {\n    paymentPayload: DeepReadonly<PaymentPayload>;\n    requirements: DeepReadonly<PaymentRequirements>;\n    declaredExtensions: DeepReadonly<Record<string, unknown>>;\n    transportContext?: unknown;\n}\ninterface VerifyResultContext extends VerifyContext {\n    result: DeepReadonly<VerifyResponse>;\n}\n/**\n * Optional acknowledgement body returned to the caller when an `AfterVerifyHook`\n * requests that the resource handler be skipped for a self-contained operation\n * (e.g. cooperative refund). Travels in-process only — never on the facilitator wire.\n */\ninterface SkipHandlerDirective {\n    contentType?: string;\n    body?: unknown;\n########## ROUTECONFIG\n */\ninterface PaywallProvider {\n    generateHtml(paymentRequired: PaymentRequired, config?: PaywallConfig): string;\n}\n/**\n * Dynamic payTo function that receives HTTP request context\n */\ntype DynamicPayTo = (context: HTTPRequestContext) => string | Promise<string>;\n/**\n * Dynamic price function that receives HTTP request context\n */\ntype DynamicPrice = (context: HTTPRequestContext) => Price | Promise<Price>;\n/**\n * Result of response body callbacks containing content type and body.\n */\ninterface HTTPResponseBody {\n    /**\n     * The content type for the response (e.g., 'application/json', 'text/plain').\n     */\n    contentType: string;\n    /**\n     * The response body to include in the 402 response.\n     */\n    body: unknown;\n}\n/**\n * Dynamic function to generate a custom response for unpaid requests.\n * Receives the HTTP request context and returns the content type and body to include in the 402 response.\n */\ntype UnpaidResponseBody = (context: HTTPRequestContext) => HTTPResponseBody | Promise<HTTPResponseBody>;\n/**\n * Dynamic function to generate a custom response for settlement failures.\n * Receives the HTTP request context and settle failure result, returns the content type and body.\n */\ntype SettlementFailedResponseBody = (context: HTTPRequestContext, settleResult: Omit<ProcessSettleFailureResponse, \"response\">) => HTTPResponseBody | Promise<HTTPResponseBody>;\n/**\n * A single payment option for a route\n * Represents one way a client can pay for access to the resource\n */\ninterface PaymentOption {\n    scheme: string;\n    payTo: string | DynamicPayTo;\n    price: Price | DynamicPrice;\n    network: Network;\n    maxTimeoutSeconds?: number;\n    extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n/**\n * Route configuration for HTTP endpoints\n *\n * The 'accepts' field defines payment options for the route.\n * Can be a single PaymentOption or an array of PaymentOptions for multiple payment methods.\n */\ninterface RouteConfig {\n    accepts: PaymentOption | PaymentOption[];\n    resource?: string;\n    description?: string;\n    mimeType?: string;\n    serviceName?: string;\n    tags?: string[];\n    iconUrl?: string;\n    customPaywallHtml?: string;\n    /**\n     * Optional callback to generate a custom response for unpaid API requests.\n     * This allows servers to return preview data, error messages, or other content\n     * when a request lacks payment.\n     *\n     * For browser requests (Accept: text/html), the paywall HTML takes precedence.\n     * This callback is only used for API clients.\n     *\n     * If not provided, defaults to { contentType: 'application/json', body: {} }.\n     *\n     * @param context - The HTTP request context\n     * @returns An object containing both contentType and body for the 402 response\n     */\n    unpaidResponseBody?: UnpaidResponseBody;\n    /**\n     * Optional callback to generate a custom response for settlement failures.\n     * If not provided, defaults to { contentType: 'application/json', body: {} }.\n     *\n     * @param context - The HTTP request context\n     * @param settleResult - The settlement failure result\n     * @returns An object containing both contentType and body for the 402 response\n     */\n    settlementFailedResponseBody?: SettlementFailedResponseBody;\n    extensions?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n/**\n * Routes configuration - maps path patterns to route configs\n */\ntype RoutesConfig = Record<string, RouteConfig> | RouteConfig;\n/**\n * Check if any routes in the configuration declare bazaar extensions.\n *\n * @param routes - Route configuration\n * @returns True if any route has extensions.bazaar defined\n */\ndeclare function checkIfBazaarNeeded(routes: {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CbJtjEH5JAD6XiEj8m9YNr","type":"tool_result","content":"import * as _x402_core_types from '@x402/core/types';\nimport { SchemeNetworkClient, PaymentRequirements, PaymentPayloadContext, PaymentPayloadResult } from '@x402/core/types';\nimport { C as ClientEvmSigner } from './signer-CJuc15ii.js';\nimport { E as EvmSchemeOptions } from './rpc-BBJ9foT3.js';\nimport { E as ExactDefaultAssetInfo } from './defaultAssets-39aDn897.js';\n\n/**\n * EVM client implementation for the Exact payment scheme.\n * Supports both EIP-3009 (transferWithAuthorization) and Permit2 flows.\n *\n * Routes to the appropriate authorization method based on\n * `requirements.extra.assetTransferMethod`. Defaults to EIP-3009\n * for backward compatibility with older facilitators.\n *\n * When the server advertises `eip2612GasSponsoring` and the asset transfer\n * method is `permit2`, the scheme automatically signs an EIP-2612 permit\n * if the user lacks Permit2 approval. This requires `readContract` on the signer.\n */\ndeclare class ExactEvmScheme implements SchemeNetworkClient {\n    private readonly signer;\n    private readonly options?;\n    readonly scheme = \"exact\";\n    findDefaultAsset: _x402_core_types.FindDefaultAsset<ExactDefaultAssetInfo>;\n    /**\n     * Creates a new ExactEvmClient instance.\n     *\n     * @param signer - regarding scheme/network implementation details\n     * @param extensionKeys - Extension keys supported by the facilitator (unused)\n     * @returns Payment requirements ready to be sent to clients\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRuuvWuGSQ7BHhTgiYtH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CEyZbdDVgQmkLWChgYLrxZ","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /tmp/sel.mjs <<'EOF'\nimport { createPublicClient, http, toFunctionSelector } from 'viem'\nconst c = createPublicClient({ transport: http('https://mainnet.base.org') })\nconst SLOT='0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc5'\nfor (const [name, addr] of [['Identity','0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432'],['Reputation','0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63']]) {\n  const raw = await c.getStorageAt({ address: addr, slot: SLOT })\n  const impl = '0x'+raw.slice(26)\n  const code = await c.getCode({ address: impl })\n  console.log(name, 'proxy', addr, 'impl', impl, 'codeLen', code.length)\n  const found = new Set()\n  // crude selector scan: PUSH4 xxxxxxxx\n  const hex = code.slice(2)\n  for (let i=0;i<hex.length-10;i+=2){ if (hex.slice(i,i+2)==='63') found.add('0x'+hex.slice(i+2,i+10)) }\n  const cands = [\n   'register()','register(string)','register(string,(address,uint256)[])','register(address)','register(string,address)',\n   'tokenURI(uint256)','ownerOf(uint256)','setTokenURI(uint256,string)','setAgentURI(uint256,string)',\n   'giveFeedback(uint256,uint8,uint8,bytes32,bytes32,string,string,bytes32)',\n   'giveFeedback(uint256,uint64,uint8,bytes32,bytes32,string,string,bytes32)',\n   'getSummary(uint256,address[],bytes32,bytes32)',\n   'getSummary(uint256,address[],bytes32,bytes32,bool)',\n   'revokeFeedback(uint256,uint64)','readFeedback(uint256,address,uint64)','getClients(uint256)',\n  ]\n  for (const s of cands){ const sel=toFunctionSelector(s); if(found.has(sel)) console.log('   HIT', sel, s) }\n}\nEOF\nnode /tmp/sel.mjs","timeout":120000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":760,"cache_read_input_tokens":62804,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":760},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"635ebbb8-ec11-49c2-8936-873cfd0c9964","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:29:20.314Z","request_id":"req_011CeRuuujmbXE6uojpaGwix"}

cat > $W/sel2.mjs <<'EOF'\nimport { createPublicClient, http, toFunctionSelector } from 'viem'\nconst c = createPublicClient({ transport: http('https://mainnet.base.org') })\nconst impl = { Identity:'0x7274e874ca62410a93bd8bf61c69d8045e399c02', Reputation:'0x16e0fa7f7c56b9a767e34b192b51f921be31da34' }\nconst sels = {}\nfor (const [n,a] of Object.entries(impl)) {\n  const hex = (await c.getCode({address:a})).slice(2); const f=new Set()\n  for (let i=0;i<hex.length-10;i+=2) if (hex.slice(i,i+2)==='63') f.add('0x'+hex.slice(i+2,i+10))\n  sels[n]=f\n}\nconst cands=[]\nconst vals=['uint8','uint64','uint128','uint256','int8','int64','int256']\nconst decs=['uint8','uint256']\nfor (const v of vals) for (const d of decs) {\n  cands.push(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},bytes32,bytes32,string,string,bytes32)`)\n  cands.push(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},string,string,string,string,bytes32)`)\n  cands.push(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},bytes32,bytes32,bytes32,string,bytes32)`)\n  cands.push(`getSummary(uint256,address[],bytes32,bytes32)`)\n}\ncands.push('getSummary(uint256,address[],bytes32,bytes32)','getSummary(uint256,address[],string,string)','getSummary(uint256,address[])',\n 'register(string,(uint256,address)[])','register(string,address)','registrations(uint256)','agentURI(uint256)',\n 'getIdentityRegistry()','feedbackCount(uint256,address)','getLastIndex(uint256,address)')\nconst uniq=[...new Set(cands)]\nfor (const [n,f] of Object.entries(sels)) { console.log('==',n)\n  for (const s of uniq) { const x=toFunctionSelector(s); if (f.has(x)) console.log('  HIT',x,s) } }\nEOF\ncd $W && node sel2.mjs","timeout":120000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":917,"cache_read_input_tokens":66925,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":917},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"63500e1d-1d27-4586-8c7f-66eeb6e211a4","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:29:45.290Z","request_id":"req_011CeRuwxRJM3mJpF4GEqXp2"}

cat > $W/sel3.mjs <<'EOF'\nimport { createPublicClient, http, toFunctionSelector } from 'viem'\nconst c = createPublicClient({ transport: http('https://mainnet.base.org') })\nconst hex = (await c.getCode({address:'0x16e0fa7f7c56b9a767e34b192b51f921be31da34'})).slice(2)\nconst f=new Set(); for (let i=0;i<hex.length-10;i+=2) if (hex.slice(i,i+2)==='63') f.add('0x'+hex.slice(i+2,i+10))\nconst vals=['uint8','uint64','uint96','uint128','uint256','int64','int128','int256']\nconst decs=['uint8','uint256']\nconst last=['bytes32','string','bytes']\nconst cands=new Set()\nfor (const v of vals) for (const d of decs) for (const l of last) {\n  cands.add(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},string,string,string,string,${l})`)\n  cands.add(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},string,string,string,${l})`)\n  cands.add(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},string,string,${l})`)\n  cands.add(`giveFeedback(uint256,${v},${d},string,string,string,string,${l},bytes)`)\n}\nfor (const s of cands) { const x=toFunctionSelector(s); if (f.has(x)) console.log('HIT',x,s) }\nconsole.log('total selectors in code:', f.size)\nEOF\ncd $W && node sel3.mjs","timeout":120000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1198,"cache_read_input_tokens":67842,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1198},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"4e0f3008-b9b2-46fa-aff4-b68e7567668c","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:29:56.612Z","request_id":"req_011CeRuxuzj6Yk5CewWCn175"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvRow72UM42PuaE3QPC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now writing the design doc and server."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1796,"cache_read_input_tokens":114032,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1796},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"abc7ac25-ae3e-4f0c-90f9-59eed5b47a5b","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:35:52.864Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvRnrs3XMqc2eq7MYAN"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvRow72UM42PuaE3QPC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FJEq1BfuCzLVTVnS6eeWoj","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/design.md <<'MARKDOWN'\n# forecast.example.com — service-side design\n\nA weather-forecast API sold to autonomous agents at **$0.35 per call**, settled in\n**native USDC on Base**. No accounts, no API keys, no invoices, no human in any step.\n\nThree standards carry the whole thing:\n\n| Need | Standard | Where it lives |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Be discoverable + judged by a stranger, with no catalog we own | **ERC-8004** IdentityRegistry (ERC-721) | Base `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` |\n| Charge per HTTP call, no accounts | **x402** (`402` → sign → retry) | `server.ts` + a facilitator |\n| Caller has USDC but no ETH | **EIP-3009** `transferWithAuthorization` | USDC itself |\n| Caller rates us afterwards, others read it before choosing | **ERC-8004** ReputationRegistry | Base `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` |\n\nEverything below marked \"verified\" was read off Base mainnet, not from memory.\n\n---\n\n## 1. The end-to-end flow\n\n```\n                       ┌─────────────────────── no human anywhere in this loop ──────────────────────┐\n client agent                                                                                        │\n   │                                                                                                 │\n   │ 1. discovery      IdentityRegistry.tokenURI(agentId)  ──►  registration file (agentURI)          │\n   │ 2. trust check    ReputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId, [clients it trusts], \"quality\", \"\")     │\n   │ 3. GET /forecast?lat=..&lon=..                                                                   │\n   │            ◄── 402 + PAYMENT-REQUIRED header (+ JSON body echoing agentId/registry)             │\n   │ 4. signs EIP-3009 transferWithAuthorization offchain (no gas, no ETH)                            │\n   │ 5. GET /forecast + PAYMENT-SIGNATURE                                                             │\n   │        server → facilitator.verify → handler runs → facilitator.settle (facilitator pays gas)    │\n   │            ◄── 200 + forecast JSON + receipt + PAYMENT-RESPONSE header (settlement tx hash)      │\n   │ 6. GET /receipts/:nonce  → exact giveFeedback(...) args + ABI-encoded calldata                   │\n   │ 7. ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, 92, 0, \"quality\", \"forecast\", endpoint, uri, hash)   │\n   └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nSteps 1, 2 and 7 are chain reads/writes the client does on its own; the registries are\npublic infrastructure we neither own nor operate. Steps 3–6 are this server.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Discovery — ERC-8004 identity, not a directory we host\n\n**What we must register before anyone can find us.**\n\nCall `IdentityRegistry.register(string agentURI)` on Base from the operator wallet. It\nmints an ERC-721; **the `agentId` is the tokenId the registry assigns us** — we do not\npick a UUID, slug, or hash. Read it back from the `Registered(uint256 agentId, string\nagentURI, address owner)` event (verified: topic\n`0xca52e62c367d81bb2e328eb795f7c7ba24afb478408a26c0e201d155c449bc4a`) or from the ERC-721\n`Transfer` log in the same receipt. That number goes into `AGENT_ID` in the server env\nand into the registration file's `registrations` array.\n\nCallers address us chain-scoped: `agentRegistry: \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\"` plus `agentId`.\n\nVerified IdentityRegistry surface on Base (proxy → impl `0x7274e874ca62410a93bd8bf61c69d8045e399c02`):\n\n```\nregister()                                   register(string)\nregister(string,(string,bytes)[])            setAgentURI(uint256,string)\nsetMetadata(uint256,string,bytes)            getMetadata(uint256,string)\nsetAgentWallet(uint256,address,uint256,bytes)\ntokenURI(uint256)  ownerOf(uint256)  isAuthorizedOrOwner(address,uint256)\nevents: Registered(uint256,string,address)   URIUpdated(uint256,string,address)\n        MetadataSet(uint256,string,string,bytes)   Transfer(address,address,uint256)\n```\n\nBoth registries are **ERC-1967 proxies** — if a signature ever surprises you, read the\nimplementation slot before assuming the ABI moved.\n\n### 2.1 What we publish, and where\n\n**`https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json`** — served by\n`server.ts`, and it is also the value we pass to `register(agentURI)`. Shape (matches the\n`registration-v1` documents already live on Base):\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"forecast.example.com\",\n  \"description\": \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35/call, USDC on Base, x402.\",\n  \"image\": \"https://forecast.example.com/icon.png\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"web\",         \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com\" },\n    { \"name\": \"x402\",        \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/forecast\" },\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\",         \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\" },\n    { \"name\": \"agentWallet\", \"endpoint\": \"eip155:8453:0x<PAY_TO>\" }\n  ],\n  \"registrations\": [\n    { \"agentId\": <tokenId>, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" }\n  ],\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\"]\n}\n```\n\n`x402Support: true` is the flag a scanning agent filters on to know it can pay us without\nan account.\n\n### 2.2 Binding the endpoint domain — the step that usually gets skipped\n\nThe endpoints we advertise (`https://forecast.example.com/forecast`) must be provably ours\nand not something anyone can point an onchain record at. There are two ways to close this\nand you have to say which one you are using.\n\n**We use: same-domain agentURI.** `tokenURI(agentId)` resolves to\n`https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json`, i.e. the file lives on\nthe same domain that serves the paid endpoints, so control of the endpoint domain is\nproven by control of the agentURI itself. The check a client runs is bidirectional:\nonchain `tokenURI` → our domain, and the file's `registrations[]` → back to\n`(agentId, eip155:8453:0x8004A169…)`. Both must agree.\n\n**If you switch the agentURI to `ipfs://` or a `data:` URI**, that proof disappears and you\nmust then publish `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json`\nseparately, carrying at minimum a `registrations` entry whose `agentRegistry` and `agentId`\nmatch the onchain agent. `server.ts` serves that path either way, so flipping the agentURI\nto IPFS does not break the binding.\n\nAn A2A `agent-card.json` is a **different document** and does not bind anything. We serve\none at `/.well-known/agent-card.json` as a capability descriptor only.\n\n### 2.3 Secondary discovery (optional, not load-bearing)\n\nThe paid route declares an **x402 Bazaar** discovery extension\n(`declareDiscoveryExtension` from `@x402/extensions/bazaar`), so a facilitator that\ncatalogs resources can index the route's input/output schema. This is a Coinbase-operated\nindex — it is a convenience, not the trust root. Discovery works with it switched off; the\nIdentityRegistry is the channel we rely on.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Payment — x402 over EIP-3009\n\n### 3.1 Wire protocol\n\n`GET /forecast` → `402` with a `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header (base64 `PaymentRequired`) → the\nclient signs → retries with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` → we verify, serve, and settle → `200`\nwith `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` (base64 `SettleResponse`, containing the settlement `transaction`\nhash). Header names verified in `@x402/core` 2.23.\n\n### 3.2 Price and asset — the two things that are easy to get wrong\n\n```ts\nprice: {\n  asset:  \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", // native USDC on Base\n  amount: \"350000\",                                     // $0.35 in base units, USDC = 6 decimals\n  extra:  { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" },           // EIP-712 domain of that token\n}\nnetwork: \"eip155:8453\"\n```\n\n- `350000`, not `0.35`, and not an 18-decimal figure. Amounts go on the wire in token base units.\n- `0x833589fC…2913` is **native** USDC on Base. `0xA0b86991…eB48` is Ethereum-mainnet USDC\n  and `0xd9aAEc86…b6CA` is bridged USDbC — both are the wrong contract for a Base route.\n- `extra.name`/`extra.version` are mandatory when the price is given as an explicit\n  `AssetAmount`. Verified in `@x402/evm`: `parsePrice()` passes an `AssetAmount` straight\n  through (`extra: price.extra || {}`) and only the `\"$0.35\"`-style money path auto-fills\n  the EIP-712 domain. Omit them and the client signs `transferWithAuthorization` against\n  the wrong domain separator and the facilitator rejects every payment.\n\nVerified against the SDK's own default asset table for `eip155:8453`:\n`{ asset: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\", decimals: 6, symbol: \"USDC\" }`,\nwith no `assetTransferMethod` override — so the exact scheme uses the **EIP-3009** path,\nnot Permit2.\n\n### 3.3 Why the caller needs no ETH\n\nUSDC implements **EIP-3009**. The client signs a `transferWithAuthorization`\n(`from`, `to`, `value`, `validAfter`, `validBefore`, `nonce`) offchain as EIP-712 typed\ndata and puts it in the `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` header. The **facilitator** submits that\nauthorization onchain and pays the gas. The caller never holds ETH and never sends a\ntransaction to pay us.\n\nThis is the mechanism — not an ERC-4337 bundler, not an unnamed \"relayer\", not\nERC-2612 `permit` followed by `transferFrom`.\n\n### 3.4 Facilitator\n\n`@coinbase/x402` exports a ready `FacilitatorConfig` (`facilitator`), wrapped in\n`HTTPFacilitatorClient`. It performs `verify` and `settle`, which means **we run no node\nand hold no gas**. Set `CDP_API_KEY_ID` / `CDP_API_KEY_SECRET` for mainnet settlement\n(`createFacilitatorConfig(id, secret)`); Coinbase's facilitator sponsors the gas and\nsweeps the USDC to `PAY_TO`.\n\nSwappable: `X402_FACILITATOR_URL` points `HTTPFacilitatorClient` at a self-hosted\nfacilitator instead. Nothing else in the design changes — this is the one dependency that\nis a business relationship rather than a public contract, and it is deliberately\none-line-replaceable.\n\n### 3.5 Settlement ordering\n\nFor the `exact` scheme settlement runs **after** the handler (`SettlePhase:\n\"after-handler\"`). Consequence: the handler can name the payer (decoded from\n`PAYMENT-SIGNATURE`) but not the settlement tx hash. The client reads the hash from the\n`PAYMENT-RESPONSE` header on the same response; we also record it in an `onAfterSettle`\nhook so `GET /receipts/:nonce` can serve it afterwards.\n\nIf the handler throws, the middleware cancels the verified payment rather than settling —\na failed forecast is not billed.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Reputation — client-attested, onchain, ours to neither write nor censor\n\n### 4.1 What the caller does\n\nAfter a paid call the client calls, on Base, from the **same address that paid**:\n\n```solidity\ngiveFeedback(\n  uint256 agentId,        // ours\n  int128  value,          // fixed point, e.g. 92\n  uint8   valueDecimals,  // 0  →  92/100.  99.77% would be value=9977, valueDecimals=2\n  string  tag1,           // \"quality\"\n  string  tag2,           // \"forecast\"\n  string  endpoint,       // \"https://forecast.example.com/forecast\"\n  string  feedbackURI,    // our receipt URL, or a data: URI with prose\n  bytes32 feedbackHash    // keccak256 of the receipt document\n)\n```\n\nVerified selector `0x3c036a7e` = `giveFeedback(uint256,int128,uint8,string,string,string,string,bytes32)`.\nNote `value` is **`int128`**, signed — negative ratings are expressible.\n\n`GET /receipts/:nonce` hands the client every one of those arguments pre-filled plus the\nABI-encoded calldata, so leaving a rating is one `eth_sendTransaction` with no human\ndeciding anything.\n\n### 4.2 We cannot rate ourselves\n\nVerified by simulating the call on Base: `giveFeedback` from `ownerOf(agentId)` reverts\nwith **`Self-feedback not allowed`**; the same call from an unrelated address succeeds.\nThe registry enforces this against the agent's owner and operators. Reputation here is\ngenuinely client-attested — which is the point, and the reason we do not host a reviews\ntable.\n\nWe *can* reply publicly: `appendResponse(uint256 agentId, address client, uint64 index,\nstring uri, bytes32 hash)` lets us attach a response to a specific feedback entry.\n\n### 4.3 How a stranger reads it — and the Sybil trap\n\n```\ngetSummary(uint256 agentId, address[] clientAddresses, string tag1, string tag2)\n        → (uint64 count, int128 averageValue, uint8 averageValueDecimals)\n```\n\nVerified selector `0x81bbba58`; return shape verified by decoding a live call\n(agent 9 → `count=15, averageValue=54, decimals=0`, matching the mean of its 15 raw scores).\n**Tags are `string`, not `bytes32`.**\n\nThe registry *refuses* to compute an unfiltered total: passing an empty `clientAddresses`\nreverts with `clientAddresses required`. The design leans on that. A client agent should:\n\n1. `getClients(agentId)` → every address that has ever rated us,\n2. intersect with addresses it independently trusts (its own past counterparties, an\n   allowlist it maintains, agents it has itself rated well),\n3. `getSummary(agentId, thatFilteredSet, \"quality\", \"forecast\")`.\n\nAnyone quoting an unfiltered average is quoting a number we could have manufactured with\n20 throwaway wallets. `readAllFeedback(uint256, address[], string, string, bool)` gives the\nraw per-entry data for a client that wants to do its own weighting;\n`readFeedback(uint256, address, uint64) → (int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1,\nstring tag2, bool isRevoked)` reads a single entry; `revokeFeedback(uint256, uint64)` lets a\nclient retract.\n\nIndexers can follow\n`NewFeedback(uint256 indexed agentId, address indexed client, string indexed tag1, uint64 index,\nint128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string feedbackURI,\nbytes32 feedbackHash)` — topic `0x6a4a61743519c9d648a14e6493f47dbe3ff1aa29e7785c96c8326a205e58febc`,\nverified by decoding a live log (tag1 appears both hashed in topic 3 and plain in the data).\n\n`GET /reputation` on this server mirrors the `getSummary` read as a convenience. It is\nexplicitly **not** authoritative and says so in its own response body: the chain is the\nsource, and a client that trusts our mirror has learned nothing it could not have been fed.\n\n### 4.4 The one gap: the rater needs gas\n\nPaying us needs no ETH (§3.3), but `giveFeedback` is a real transaction on Base and someone\nmust pay its gas. We cannot pay it — the self-feedback ban means we would have to be the\nsender, and a sender who is us is rejected. This is the client's problem, and it has a\nclean answer worth stating because it interacts with §4.3:\n\n**EIP-7702.** The client's EOA signs a type-`0x04` authorization *with its own key*, writing\n`0xef0100 || implementation` into its own account, and a sponsor then submits the\n`giveFeedback` call with gas paid by the sponsor. Live since Pectra, 2025-05-07. The\ndecisive property is that the client's **address does not change** — which matters here\nspecifically, because the address x402 recorded as the payer is the address `getSummary`\nfilters on. Migrating to a fresh ERC-4337 smart account would put the client at a *new*\naddress, orphaning its payment history from its rating history and making it invisible to\nevery trust filter built on §4.3. The delegation persists until replaced or cleared by\ndelegating to the zero address; it is not per-transaction.\n\nFailing that, the client keeps a few cents of ETH on Base. Either way it is one signature,\nno human.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Dependency ledger\n\n### Onchain (Base, `eip155:8453`)\n\n| Contract | Address | We depend on it for | Trust |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` | `register`, `tokenURI`, `ownerOf` | ERC-1967 proxy, owner-upgradeable — not ours |\n| ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` | `giveFeedback`, `getSummary`, `getClients`, `appendResponse` | ERC-1967 proxy, owner-upgradeable — not ours |\n| USDC (native, Base) | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` | EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`, EIP-712 domain `(\"USD Coin\",\"2\")` | Circle, upgradeable |\n\nBoth `0x8004…` addresses are the **mainnet** pair and are deployed on Base (verified:\nnon-empty `eth_getCode`). The **testnet pair is different** — `0x8004A818BFB912233c491871b3d84c89A494BD9e`\n(Identity) and `0x8004B663056A597Dffe9eCcC1965A193B7388713` (Reputation). The mainnet\naddresses have **no code** on Base Sepolia, so a staging deploy that reuses them fails at\nthe first call. `NETWORK` and both registry addresses are env-driven for that reason.\n\nThe ERC-8004 EIP is still **Draft** even though the contracts are live. Anything not\nverified against the deployed bytecode should be treated as movable.\n\n### Offchain\n\n| Dependency | Role | If it fails |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| x402 facilitator (Coinbase, or self-hosted) | `verify` + `settle`, pays settlement gas | Payments stop. Swap `X402_FACILITATOR_URL` / run your own. **Only non-public-good dependency.** |\n| Base RPC | `/reputation` mirror only | Mirror degrades; paid path unaffected — the server never touches an RPC to sell a forecast |\n| `forecast.example.com` DNS + TLS | Serves the agentURI target → the domain binding (§2.2) | Discovery and binding break. Registry entry survives; move the agentURI to IPFS and keep serving `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` |\n| Upstream weather source | The actual product | Handler throws → payment is cancelled, not settled |\n| `PAY_TO` wallet | Receives USDC | Rotate via `setAgentWallet` + a new agentURI |\n\n### Not used, and why\n\n- **ERC-4337** — nothing here needs a smart account, and for the *client* it is the wrong\n  answer (§4.4: new address, orphaned reputation).\n- **ERC-2612 `permit` + `transferFrom`** — two steps and an allowance where EIP-3009 is one\n  signature. USDC supports 3009 natively; use it.\n- **ERC-8004 Validation Registry** — the spec defines one (re-execution, zkML, TEE\n  attestation, 0–100 validator scores) but it is *not* part of the deployed pair above.\n  Verify it exists onchain before designing on it. Reputation alone carries the trust story here.\n- **An API-key tier / a reviews table we host** — both are the wrong answer to problems\n  x402 and ERC-8004 already solve, and a self-hosted reviews table is exactly the \"catalog\n  you own\" the requirements rule out.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Publish / register checklist\n\n1. Fund `PAY_TO` — it needs **no ETH**; the facilitator pays settlement gas. It only receives USDC.\n2. Deploy `server.ts` behind `https://forecast.example.com` (TLS required — the agentURI is an `https://` URL).\n3. Confirm `GET /.well-known/agent-registration.json` returns 200 with `agentId: null`.\n4. From the operator wallet (this one *does* need a little ETH on Base, once), call\n   `IdentityRegistry.register(\"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json\")`.\n5. Read `agentId` from the `Registered` event; set `AGENT_ID` in the server env and restart.\n6. Verify the round trip: `tokenURI(agentId)` → our URL → the file's `registrations[]`\n   → back to `(agentId, eip155:8453:0x8004A169…)`. If those disagree, no client should trust us, and correctly won't.\n7. Optional: `setMetadata(agentId, \"x402\", …)` for registry-level indexing hints.\n8. Optional: register the route with an x402 Bazaar-capable facilitator (§2.3).\n\nNothing in this list has to happen twice, and after step 5 no step in the serving path\ntouches a human.\n\n## 7. Env\n\n```\nPAY_TO=0x…                 # USDC recipient\nAGENT_ID=…                 # tokenId from register(); until set, endpoints report unregistered\nORIGIN=https://forecast.example.com\nNETWORK=eip155:8453\nUSDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nIDENTITY_REGISTRY=0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\nREPUTATION_REGISTRY=0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\nCDP_API_KEY_ID= / CDP_API_KEY_SECRET=      # Coinbase facilitator (mainnet settlement)\nX402_FACILITATOR_URL=                      # or point at your own\nBASE_RPC_URL=https://mainnet.base.org      # /reputation mirror only\n```\nMARKDOWN\necho written"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1796,"cache_read_input_tokens":114032,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1796},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"06fb7631-6883-4021-862b-7aeff8b2973c","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:37:42.749Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvRnrs3XMqc2eq7MYAN"}

7AWz0K7sOd9W77dpZBUohCyIktF+jii1fsqAHAmQzV/fGsPn5BIgMn1g0tKBlyDxzLZEWmYj5cs8wfWYjLseWDIYj88zjAOLDHgqmucgf/JLRCM+5apzpxIJU9zpHF7YdC+MJoXcxa0n6cHoleJzCs1D4bzbeh9ZPsIn2UYipZIljp+cESZYJwi4CjCFzLkIf28NjuPoyniIHTBA5gatDJQleVdcPDwRTpcttCH4a285EXwi/OW4O2EeRSWdBDGvDxJG1McPcdY/ftgGtSyvp9qhsdzbTFNAxCtlpaoA4bKhCltcQZU5CaGMoIROkSq8Jte/8z3fFt0Oe7NWqMA5GoeE4o4/06JcqORmt2xLP3sl6QuuXywiDOPdjIbiLKjhvOQE6pzRmx5CNJ5vJIDpnOIn7K0TUvJ1ePgYAQ=="}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8347,"cache_read_input_tokens":115828,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":8347},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"d9200157-df03-4aaa-a767-ca1c322467e7","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:37:53.434Z","request_id":"req_011CeRva2tgeezeK1o65noS2"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRva3ux8V1fSUY6toQeV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NLxdVm172ftPXb9FWksdKQ","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/server.ts <<'TYPESCRIPT'\n/**\n * forecast.example.com — service side.\n *\n * Sells point weather forecasts to autonomous agents for $0.35/call, settled in\n * native USDC on Base via x402. No accounts, no API keys, no human.\n *\n * See design.md. In short:\n *   - discovery + identity : ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry (Base 0x8004A169…)\n *   - payment              : x402, exact scheme, eip155:8453\n *   - caller needs no ETH  : USDC's EIP-3009 transferWithAuthorization, gas paid by the facilitator\n *   - reputation           : ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry (Base 0x8004BAa1…), client-attested\n */\n\nimport express, { type Request, type Response } from \"express\";\nimport { paymentMiddleware } from \"@x402/express\";\nimport {\n  HTTPFacilitatorClient,\n  x402ResourceServer,\n  type RoutesConfig,\n} from \"@x402/core/server\";\nimport { decodePaymentSignatureHeader } from \"@x402/core/http\";\nimport type { Network } from \"@x402/core/types\";\nimport { registerExactEvmScheme } from \"@x402/evm/exact/server\";\nimport { declareDiscoveryExtension } from \"@x402/extensions/bazaar\";\nimport { createFacilitatorConfig } from \"@coinbase/x402\";\nimport {\n  createPublicClient,\n  encodeFunctionData,\n  http as viemHttp,\n  keccak256,\n  stringToHex,\n} from \"viem\";\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Config\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 4021);\nconst ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\n\n/** CAIP-2. Base mainnet. The ERC-8004 pair below is the *mainnet* pair — see design.md §5. */\nconst NETWORK = (process.env.NETWORK ?? \"eip155:8453\") as Network;\n\n/**\n * Native USDC on Base. Not 0xA0b86991…eB48 (Ethereum mainnet USDC) and not\n * 0xd9aAEc86…b6CA (bridged USDbC) — both are the wrong contract for a Base route.\n */\nconst USDC = (process.env.USDC_ADDRESS ??\n  \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/** $0.35 in token base units. USDC has 6 decimals, so 0.35 → 350000. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_LABEL = \"$0.35\";\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = (process.env.IDENTITY_REGISTRY ??\n  \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\") as `0x${string}`;\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = (process.env.REPUTATION_REGISTRY ??\n  \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/** USDC recipient. Needs no ETH — the facilitator pays settlement gas. */\nconst PAY_TO = (process.env.PAY_TO ??\n  \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/**\n * The ERC-721 tokenId the IdentityRegistry assigned us at register(). Not a UUID,\n * slug or hash we chose. Unset until step 5 of the checklist in design.md §6.\n */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ? BigInt(process.env.AGENT_ID) : null;\n\nconst AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP = `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`;\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY_CAIP = `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`;\nconst FORECAST_ENDPOINT = `${ORIGIN}/forecast`;\n\nconst BASE_RPC_URL = process.env.BASE_RPC_URL ?? \"https://mainnet.base.org\";\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// ERC-8004 ABI fragments — signatures verified against the deployed Base bytecode\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/** selector 0x3c036a7e. `value` is int128 (signed): negative ratings are expressible. */\nconst GIVE_FEEDBACK_ABI = [\n  {\n    type: \"function\",\n    name: \"giveFeedback\",\n    stateMutability: \"nonpayable\",\n    inputs: [\n      { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n      { name: \"value\", type: \"int128\" },\n      { name: \"valueDecimals\", type: \"uint8\" },\n      { name: \"tag1\", type: \"string\" },\n      { name: \"tag2\", type: \"string\" },\n      { name: \"endpoint\", type: \"string\" },\n      { name: \"feedbackURI\", type: \"string\" },\n      { name: \"feedbackHash\", type: \"bytes32\" },\n    ],\n    outputs: [],\n  },\n] as const;\n\n/** selector 0x81bbba58. Tags are `string`, not bytes32. Reverts if clientAddresses is empty. */\nconst REPUTATION_READ_ABI = [\n  {\n    type: \"function\",\n    name: \"getSummary\",\n    stateMutability: \"view\",\n    inputs: [\n      { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n      { name: \"clientAddresses\", type: \"address[]\" },\n      { name: \"tag1\", type: \"string\" },\n      { name: \"tag2\", type: \"string\" },\n    ],\n    outputs: [\n      { name: \"count\", type: \"uint64\" },\n      { name: \"averageValue\", type: \"int128\" },\n      { name: \"averageValueDecimals\", type: \"uint8\" },\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    type: \"function\",\n    name: \"getClients\",\n    stateMutability: \"view\",\n    inputs: [{ name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" }],\n    outputs: [{ name: \"clients\", type: \"address[]\" }],\n  },\n] as const;\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// x402 wiring\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * The facilitator runs verify + settle, so this service never runs a node and never\n * holds gas. Coinbase's by default; X402_FACILITATOR_URL points at your own instead.\n */\nconst facilitatorClient = new HTTPFacilitatorClient(\n  process.env.X402_FACILITATOR_URL\n    ? { url: process.env.X402_FACILITATOR_URL }\n    : createFacilitatorConfig(\n        process.env.CDP_API_KEY_ID,\n        process.env.CDP_API_KEY_SECRET,\n      ),\n);\n\nconst resourceServer = new x402ResourceServer(facilitatorClient);\nregisterExactEvmScheme(resourceServer, { networks: [NETWORK] });\n\nconst routes: RoutesConfig = {\n  \"GET /forecast\": {\n    resource: FORECAST_ENDPOINT,\n    serviceName: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: `Point weather forecast for a lat/lon. ${PRICE_LABEL} per call, USDC on Base.`,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"geo\"],\n    accepts: [\n      {\n        scheme: \"exact\",\n        network: NETWORK,\n        payTo: PAY_TO,\n        price: {\n          asset: USDC,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          // EIP-712 domain of *this* token. Mandatory when the price is an explicit\n          // AssetAmount — the SDK only auto-fills it on the \"$0.35\"-style money path.\n          // Wrong or missing → the client signs transferWithAuthorization against the\n          // wrong domain separator and every payment is rejected.\n          extra: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" },\n        },\n        maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n      },\n    ],\n    // Optional secondary discovery: lets a Bazaar-capable facilitator catalog the\n    // route's shape. Not the trust root — that is the IdentityRegistry (design.md §2.3).\n    extensions: declareDiscoveryExtension({\n      input: { lat: \"52.52\", lon: \"13.405\", hours: 24 },\n      inputSchema: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        required: [\"lat\", \"lon\"],\n        properties: {\n          lat: { type: \"number\", minimum: -90, maximum: 90 },\n          lon: { type: \"number\", minimum: -180, maximum: 180 },\n          hours: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 },\n        },\n      },\n      output: {\n        example: {\n          location: { lat: 52.52, lon: 13.405 },\n          issuedAt: \"2026-08-26T19:00:00.000Z\",\n          hourly: [{ t: \"2026-08-26T20:00:00.000Z\", tempC: 18.4, precipMm: 0, windMs: 3.1 }],\n        },\n      },\n    }),\n    // A first-time caller gets the identity + reputation pointers in the 402 itself,\n    // so it can decide whether we are worth paying before it signs anything.\n    unpaidResponseBody: async () => ({\n      contentType: \"application/json\",\n      body: {\n        error: \"payment required\",\n        price: { amount: PRICE_ATOMIC, decimals: 6, asset: USDC, network: NETWORK, display: PRICE_LABEL },\n        identity: identityPointer(),\n        howToVerifyUs: howToVerifyUs(),\n      },\n    }),\n  },\n};\n\n/** Receipt store, keyed by the EIP-3009 authorization nonce (unique per payment). */\ntype Receipt = {\n  id: string;\n  payer: string | null;\n  amount: string;\n  asset: string;\n  network: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  requestedAt: string;\n  settlement: { transaction: string; success: boolean; settledAt: string } | null;\n};\nconst RECEIPT_LIMIT = 10_000;\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n\nfunction putReceipt(r: Receipt) {\n  if (receipts.size >= RECEIPT_LIMIT) {\n    const oldest = receipts.keys().next();\n    if (!oldest.done) receipts.delete(oldest.value);\n  }\n  receipts.set(r.id, r);\n}\n\n/**\n * The `exact` scheme settles *after* the handler, so the handler cannot know the\n * settlement tx hash. The client reads it from the PAYMENT-RESPONSE header on its own\n * 200; we capture it here so GET /receipts/:id can serve it later.\n */\nresourceServer.onAfterSettle(async (ctx) => {\n  const id = paymentId(ctx.paymentPayload.payload as Record<string, unknown>);\n  if (!id) return;\n  const existing = receipts.get(id);\n  const settlement = {\n    transaction: ctx.result.transaction,\n    success: ctx.result.success,\n    settledAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n  if (existing) existing.settlement = settlement;\n  else\n    putReceipt({\n      id,\n      payer: ctx.result.payer ?? null,\n      amount: ctx.requirements.amount,\n      asset: ctx.requirements.asset,\n      network: ctx.requirements.network,\n      endpoint: FORECAST_ENDPOINT,\n      requestedAt: settlement.settledAt,\n      settlement,\n    });\n});\n\n/** EIP-3009 nonce, or the Permit2 nonce — either way, unique per payment. */\nfunction paymentId(payload: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {\n  const auth = payload?.authorization as { nonce?: string } | undefined;\n  if (auth?.nonce) return auth.nonce.toLowerCase();\n  const p2 = payload?.permit2Authorization as { nonce?: string } | undefined;\n  if (p2?.nonce) return `permit2:${p2.nonce}`;\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction payerOf(payload: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {\n  const auth = payload?.authorization as { from?: string } | undefined;\n  if (auth?.from) return auth.from;\n  const p2 = payload?.permit2Authorization as { from?: string } | undefined;\n  return p2?.from ?? null;\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Shared descriptors\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction identityPointer() {\n  return {\n    // The agentId is the ERC-721 tokenId the registry assigned. Callers address us\n    // chain-scoped: (agentRegistry, agentId).\n    agentId: AGENT_ID === null ? null : Number(AGENT_ID),\n    agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n    reputationRegistry: REPUTATION_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n    agentURI: `${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-registration.json`,\n    registered: AGENT_ID !== null,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction howToVerifyUs() {\n  return {\n    step1: `Read tokenURI(${AGENT_ID ?? \"<agentId>\"}) on ${AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP}; it must resolve to ${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-registration.json — the same domain that serves this endpoint. That is what binds these endpoints to the onchain agent.`,\n    step2: \"Check that file's registrations[] points back to this agentId and agentRegistry. If the two disagree, do not trust this service.\",\n    step3: `Read reputation onchain: getClients(agentId) on ${REPUTATION_REGISTRY_CAIP}, intersect with clients you independently trust, then getSummary(agentId, thatFilteredSet, \"quality\", \"forecast\").`,\n    warning:\n      \"getSummary reverts on an empty client set by design. An unfiltered average over all raters is Sybil bait — filter to addresses you have your own reason to trust.\",\n  };\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Server\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst app = express();\napp.use(express.json());\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\n\n/**\n * The ERC-8004 registration file. This exact URL is the agentURI we pass to\n * IdentityRegistry.register(), so the domain serving the paid endpoints is the domain\n * serving the registration file — that is how the endpoints are bound to the onchain\n * agent (design.md §2.2). If the agentURI is ever moved to ipfs:// or a data: URI, this\n * path must keep serving at least `registrations` so the binding survives.\n */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  res.type(\"application/json\").json({\n    type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35 per call, native USDC on Base, paid over x402 with no account and no API key.\",\n    image: `${ORIGIN}/icon.png`,\n    services: [\n      { name: \"web\", endpoint: ORIGIN },\n      { name: \"x402\", endpoint: FORECAST_ENDPOINT },\n      { name: \"A2A\", endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-card.json` },\n      { name: \"agentWallet\", endpoint: `${NETWORK}:${PAY_TO}` },\n    ],\n    registrations: [\n      { agentId: AGENT_ID === null ? null : Number(AGENT_ID), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP },\n    ],\n    active: true,\n    x402Support: true,\n    supportedTrust: [\"reputation\"],\n  });\n});\n\n/**\n * A2A capability card. Note this is a *different* document from the registration file\n * above and does not bind anything — it is a service descriptor only.\n */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Point weather forecasts, priced per call.\",\n    url: ORIGIN,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly forecast for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\"],\n      },\n    ],\n    identity: identityPointer(),\n  });\n});\n\n/** Machine-readable service terms. Free, so a stranger can price us before paying. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/service.json\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  res.json({\n    endpoint: FORECAST_ENDPOINT,\n    method: \"GET\",\n    payment: {\n      protocol: \"x402\",\n      scheme: \"exact\",\n      network: NETWORK,\n      asset: USDC,\n      assetSymbol: \"USDC\",\n      assetDecimals: 6,\n      amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n      display: PRICE_LABEL,\n      payTo: PAY_TO,\n      // Why a caller holding USDC but no ETH can still pay us:\n      transferMethod: \"eip3009\",\n      gasless: true,\n      gaslessNote:\n        \"USDC implements EIP-3009. Sign transferWithAuthorization offchain (EIP-712) and put it in the PAYMENT-SIGNATURE header; the facilitator submits it onchain and pays the gas. You never need ETH to pay for a call.\",\n    },\n    identity: identityPointer(),\n    howToVerifyUs: howToVerifyUs(),\n    rating: {\n      registry: REPUTATION_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n      write: \"giveFeedback(uint256,int128,uint8,string,string,string,string,bytes32)\",\n      note: `After any paid call, GET ${ORIGIN}/receipts/{id} for pre-filled arguments and ABI-encoded calldata.`,\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n// Everything below /forecast is behind payment.\napp.use(paymentMiddleware(routes, resourceServer));\n\napp.get(\"/forecast\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const lat = Number(req.query.lat);\n  const lon = Number(req.query.lon);\n  const hours = Math.min(Math.max(Number(req.query.hours ?? 24) || 24, 1), 168);\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90 || !Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) {\n    // Thrown/4xx before settlement → the verified payment is cancelled, not charged.\n    res.status(400).json({ error: \"lat and lon are required; lat ∈ [-90,90], lon ∈ [-180,180]\" });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Identify the payer from the payment header. Verification already happened in the\n  // middleware; settlement happens after this handler returns.\n  let receiptId: string | null = null;\n  let payer: string | null = null;\n  const header = req.header(\"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE\");\n  if (header) {\n    try {\n      const payload = decodePaymentSignatureHeader(header);\n      receiptId = paymentId(payload.payload);\n      payer = payerOf(payload.payload);\n    } catch {\n      /* verified upstream; a decode failure here only costs us the receipt id */\n    }\n  }\n\n  const forecast = await getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n\n  if (receiptId) {\n    putReceipt({\n      id: receiptId,\n      payer,\n      amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n      asset: USDC,\n      network: NETWORK,\n      endpoint: FORECAST_ENDPOINT,\n      requestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      settlement: receipts.get(receiptId)?.settlement ?? null,\n    });\n  }\n\n  res.json({\n    ...forecast,\n    receipt: receiptId\n      ? {\n          id: receiptId,\n          url: `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receiptId}`,\n          note: \"Settlement tx hash is in the PAYMENT-RESPONSE header of this response; the receipt URL carries it too once settlement lands.\",\n          rateUs: `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receiptId}`,\n        }\n      : null,\n  });\n});\n\n/**\n * Free. Returns the receipt plus every argument the caller needs for\n * ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback — pre-filled and ABI-encoded, so leaving a rating is\n * one transaction with no human deciding anything.\n */\napp.get(\"/receipts/:id\", (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const receipt = receipts.get(String(req.params.id).toLowerCase());\n  if (!receipt) {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n    return;\n  }\n  if (AGENT_ID === null) {\n    res.status(503).json({ error: \"not yet registered in the IdentityRegistry\", receipt });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const feedbackURI = `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receipt.id}`;\n  const feedbackHash = keccak256(stringToHex(JSON.stringify(receipt)));\n\n  const template = (value: number, valueDecimals = 0, tag1 = \"quality\", tag2 = \"forecast\") => ({\n    args: {\n      agentId: Number(AGENT_ID),\n      value,\n      valueDecimals,\n      tag1,\n      tag2,\n      endpoint: FORECAST_ENDPOINT,\n      feedbackURI,\n      feedbackHash,\n    },\n    calldata: encodeFunctionData({\n      abi: GIVE_FEEDBACK_ABI,\n      functionName: \"giveFeedback\",\n      args: [\n        AGENT_ID,\n        BigInt(value),\n        valueDecimals,\n        tag1,\n        tag2,\n        FORECAST_ENDPOINT,\n        feedbackURI,\n        feedbackHash,\n      ],\n    }),\n  });\n\n  res.json({\n    receipt,\n    rateUs: {\n      to: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      chain: NETWORK,\n      function: \"giveFeedback(uint256,int128,uint8,string,string,string,string,bytes32)\",\n      valueEncoding:\n        \"Fixed point. value=92, valueDecimals=0 is 92/100. value=9977, valueDecimals=2 is 99.77%. value is int128, so negative ratings are valid.\",\n      // Substitute your own score; the calldata below is a ready-to-send example.\n      example: template(92),\n      mustBeSentBy: receipt.payer,\n      whySender:\n        \"Send from the address that paid. getSummary filters by client address, so a rating from a fresh address is worth nothing to anyone reading it — and the registry rejects feedback from our own owner/operators (`Self-feedback not allowed`), so we cannot post it for you.\",\n      gas: {\n        note: \"Paying us needs no ETH (EIP-3009), but this rating is a real Base transaction and someone must pay its gas. We cannot: a sender who is us is rejected.\",\n        recommended:\n          \"EIP-7702 — your EOA signs a type-0x04 authorization with its own key and a sponsor submits, gas paid by the sponsor. Your address is unchanged, which matters here: the address x402 recorded as payer is the address getSummary filters on. Deploying a fresh ERC-4337 account instead would move you to a new address and orphan your payment history from your rating history.\",\n        alternative: \"Keep a few cents of ETH on Base.\",\n      },\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n/**\n * Convenience mirror of the onchain reputation read. NOT authoritative — a client that\n * trusts this endpoint has learned nothing it could not have been fed. It exists so an\n * agent without an RPC can get a first look; the response says so itself.\n *\n *   GET /reputation?clients=0xabc,0xdef[&tag1=quality][&tag2=forecast]\n */\napp.get(\"/reputation\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  if (AGENT_ID === null) {\n    res.status(503).json({ error: \"not yet registered in the IdentityRegistry\" });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const clients = String(req.query.clients ?? \"\")\n    .split(\",\")\n    .map((c) => c.trim())\n    .filter((c) => /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(c)) as `0x${string}`[];\n\n  const tag1 = String(req.query.tag1 ?? \"quality\");\n  const tag2 = String(req.query.tag2 ?? \"forecast\");\n\n  const disclaimer = {\n    authoritative: false,\n    readItYourself: {\n      chain: NETWORK,\n      contract: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      getClients: \"getClients(uint256) → address[]\",\n      getSummary:\n        \"getSummary(uint256,address[],string,string) → (uint64 count, int128 averageValue, uint8 averageValueDecimals)\",\n    },\n  };\n\n  if (clients.length === 0) {\n    res.status(400).json({\n      error: \"supply ?clients=0x…,0x… — a set of raters you independently trust\",\n      why: \"The registry itself reverts with `clientAddresses required` on an empty set. An unfiltered average over every rater is Sybil bait: we could have manufactured it with 20 throwaway wallets. Start from getClients(agentId) and intersect with addresses you have your own reason to trust.\",\n      ...disclaimer,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const publicClient = createPublicClient({ transport: viemHttp(BASE_RPC_URL) });\n    const [count, averageValue, averageValueDecimals] = await publicClient.readContract({\n      address: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      abi: REPUTATION_READ_ABI,\n      functionName: \"getSummary\",\n      args: [AGENT_ID, clients, tag1, tag2],\n    });\n    res.json({\n      agentId: Number(AGENT_ID),\n      filteredTo: clients,\n      tag1,\n      tag2,\n      count: Number(count),\n      averageValue: Number(averageValue),\n      averageValueDecimals,\n      average: Number(averageValue) / 10 ** averageValueDecimals,\n      ...disclaimer,\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    res.status(502).json({ error: \"onchain read failed\", detail: String(err), ...disclaimer });\n  }\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  res.json({ ok: true, registered: AGENT_ID !== null, network: NETWORK });\n});\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// The actual product\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number) {\n  // Replace with the real upstream. Throwing here cancels the verified payment rather\n  // than settling it, so a failed forecast is not billed.\n  const issuedAt = new Date();\n  const hourly = Array.from({ length: hours }, (_, i) => {\n    const t = new Date(issuedAt.getTime() + (i + 1) * 3_600_000);\n    return {\n      t: t.toISOString(),\n      tempC: Number((12 + 8 * Math.sin(((t.getUTCHours() - 4) / 24) * 2 * Math.PI)).toFixed(1)),\n      precipMm: 0,\n      windMs: 3.1,\n    };\n  });\n  return { location: { lat, lon }, issuedAt: issuedAt.toISOString(), hours, hourly };\n}\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  price      ${PRICE_LABEL} = ${PRICE_ATOMIC} base units of ${USDC} on ${NETWORK}`);\n  console.log(`  payTo      ${PAY_TO}`);\n  console.log(\n    AGENT_ID === null\n      ? `  agentId    UNSET — call IdentityRegistry.register(\"${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-registration.json\") on ${AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP}, then set AGENT_ID to the tokenId from the Registered event`\n      : `  agentId    ${AGENT_ID} @ ${AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP}`,\n  );\n});\n\nexport { app };\nTYPESCRIPT\necho written"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8347,"cache_read_input_tokens":115828,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":8347},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"565d30f4-964a-457d-93cf-3f423e72fe96","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:39:32.781Z","request_id":"req_011CeRva2tgeezeK1o65noS2"}

The client signs the EIP-3009\n * `transferWithAuthorization` against this, so a wrong `name`/`version` means every\n * signature is rejected. Resolved from the SDK's asset table for the configured\n * (asset, network) so it can never drift from USDC_ADDRESS; override only for a token\n * the SDK does not know. For native USDC on Base this is (\"USD Coin\", \"2\").\n */\nconst assetDefaults = findDefaultAsset(USDC, NETWORK);\nconst USDC_EIP712 = {\n  name: process.env.USDC_EIP712_NAME ?? assetDefaults?.name,\n  version: process.env.USDC_EIP712_VERSION ?? assetDefaults?.version,\n};\nif (!USDC_EIP712.name || !USDC_EIP712.version) {\n  throw new Error(\n    `No EIP-712 domain known for asset ${USDC} on ${NETWORK}. ` +\n      \"Set USDC_EIP712_NAME and USDC_EIP712_VERSION — without them the client signs \" +\n      \"transferWithAuthorization against the wrong domain separator and no payment verifies.\",\n  );\n}''')\n\ns=s.replace(\n'''          // EIP-712 domain of *this* token. Mandatory when the price is an explicit\n          // AssetAmount — the SDK only auto-fills it on the \"$0.35\"-style money path.\n          // Wrong or missing → the client signs transferWithAuthorization against the\n          // wrong domain separator and every payment is rejected.\n          extra: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" },''',\n'''          // Mandatory when the price is an explicit AssetAmount: the SDK only auto-fills\n          // the EIP-712 domain on the \"$0.35\"-style money path (verified in\n          // @x402/evm parsePrice, which passes an AssetAmount straight through).\n          extra: { name: USDC_EIP712.name, version: USDC_EIP712.version },''')\n\ns=s.replace(\n'''  console.log(`  price      ${PRICE_LABEL} = ${PRICE_ATOMIC} base units of ${USDC} on ${NETWORK}`);''',\n'''  console.log(\n    `  price      ${PRICE_LABEL} = ${PRICE_ATOMIC} base units of ${USDC} on ${NETWORK} ` +\n      `(EIP-712 domain \"${USDC_EIP712.name}\" v${USDC_EIP712.version})`,\n  );''')\n\nopen(p,\"w\").write(s)\nprint(\"patched\")\nPYEOF\ncd $W && npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | head -20","timeout":180000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2927,"cache_read_input_tokens":139899,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2927},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"ecec8ed3-5a78-40b9-a179-9e5921c10a6c","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:41:01.307Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvnqUxK1ar3QprPhLDD"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011KdGbxZPMCY864JbuWEtXy","type":"tool_result","content":"Run `npm audit` for details.\nimport { x402Client, x402HTTPClient, x402ClientConfig } from '@x402/core/client';\nexport { HTTPResourceResponse, PaymentPolicy, SchemeRegistration, SelectPaymentRequirements, x402Client, x402ClientConfig, x402HTTPClient } from '@x402/core/client';\nexport { decodePaymentResponseHeader } from '@x402/core/http';\nexport { Network, PaymentPayload, PaymentRequired, PaymentRequirements, SchemeNetworkClient } from '@x402/core/types';\n\n/**\n * Enables the payment of APIs using the x402 payment protocol v2.\n *\n * This function wraps the native fetch API to automatically handle 402 Payment Required responses\n * by creating and sending payment headers. It will:\n * 1. Make the initial request\n * 2. If a 402 response is received, parse the payment requirements\n * 3. Create a payment header using the configured x402HTTPClient\n * 4. Retry the request with the payment header\n *\n * @param fetch - The fetch function to wrap (typically globalThis.fetch)\n * @param client - Configured x402Client or x402HTTPClient instance for handling payments\n * @returns A wrapped fetch function that handles 402 responses automatically\n *\n * @example\n * ```typescript\n * import { wrapFetchWithPayment, x402Client } from '@x402/fetch';\n * import { ExactEvmScheme } from '@x402/evm';\n * import { ExactSvmScheme } from '@x402/svm';\n *\n * const client = new x402Client()\n *   .register('eip155:8453', new ExactEvmScheme(evmSigner))\n *   .register('solana:mainnet', new ExactSvmScheme(svmSigner))\n *   .register('eip155:1', new ExactEvmScheme(evmSigner), 1); // v1 protocol\n *\n * const fetchWithPay = wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch, client);\n *\n * // Make a request that may require payment\n * const response = await fetchWithPay('https://api.example.com/paid-endpoint');\n * ```\n *\n * @throws {Error} If no schemes are provided\n * @throws {Error} If the request configuration is missing\n * @throws {Error} If a payment has already been attempted for this request\n * @throws {Error} If there's an error creating the payment header\n */\ndeclare function wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch: typeof globalThis.fetch, client: x402Client | x402HTTPClient): (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;\n/**\n * Creates a payment-enabled fetch function from a configuration object.\n *\n * @param fetch - The fetch function to wrap (typically globalThis.fetch)\n * @param config - Configuration options including scheme registrations and selectors\n * @returns A wrapped fetch function that handles 402 responses automatically\n */\ndeclare function wrapFetchWithPaymentFromConfig(fetch: typeof globalThis.fetch, config: x402ClientConfig): (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;\n\nexport { wrapFetchWithPayment, wrapFetchWithPaymentFromConfig };","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"a60db4c6-e931-46d9-8a27-f4c99dae2fb1","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:41:22.689Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"Run `npm audit` for details.\nimport { x402Client, x402HTTPClient, x402ClientConfig } from '@x402/core/client';\nexport { HTTPResourceResponse, PaymentPolicy, SchemeRegistration, SelectPaymentRequirements, x402Client, x402ClientConfig, x402HTTPClient } from '@x402/core/client';\nexport { decodePaymentResponseHeader } from '@x402/core/http';\nexport { Network, PaymentPayload, PaymentRequired, PaymentRequirements, SchemeNetworkClient } from '@x402/core/types';\n\n/**\n * Enables the payment of APIs using the x402 payment protocol v2.\n *\n * This function wraps the native fetch API to automatically handle 402 Payment Required responses\n * by creating and sending payment headers. It will:\n * 1. Make the initial request\n * 2. If a 402 response is received, parse the payment requirements\n * 3. Create a payment header using the configured x402HTTPClient\n * 4. Retry the request with the payment header\n *\n * @param fetch - The fetch function to wrap (typically globalThis.fetch)\n * @param client - Configured x402Client or x402HTTPClient instance for handling payments\n * @returns A wrapped fetch function that handles 402 responses automatically\n *\n * @example\n * ```typescript\n * import { wrapFetchWithPayment, x402Client } from '@x402/fetch';\n * import { ExactEvmScheme } from '@x402/evm';\n * import { ExactSvmScheme } from '@x402/svm';\n *\n * const client = new x402Client()\n *   .register('eip155:8453', new ExactEvmScheme(evmSigner))\n *   .register('solana:mainnet', new ExactSvmScheme(svmSigner))\n *   .register('eip155:1', new ExactEvmScheme(evmSigner), 1); // v1 protocol\n *\n * const fetchWithPay = wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch, client);\n *\n * // Make a request that may require payment\n * const response = await fetchWithPay('https://api.example.com/paid-endpoint');\n * ```\n *\n * @throws {Error} If no schemes are provided\n * @throws {Error} If the request configuration is missing\n * @throws {Error} If a payment has already been attempted for this request\n * @throws {Error} If there's an error creating the payment header\n */\ndeclare function wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch: typeof globalThis.fetch, client: x402Client | x402HTTPClient): (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;\n/**\n * Creates a payment-enabled fetch function from a configuration object.\n *\n * @param fetch - The fetch function to wrap (typically globalThis.fetch)\n * @param config - Configuration options including scheme registrations and selectors\n * @returns A wrapped fetch function that handles 402 responses automatically\n */\ndeclare function wrapFetchWithPaymentFromConfig(fetch: typeof globalThis.fetch, config: x402ClientConfig): (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;\n\nexport { wrapFetchWithPayment, wrapFetchWithPaymentFromConfig };","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
