cat > /tmp/smoke.mts <<'EOF'\nimport { wrapFetchWithPayment } from \"@x402/fetch\";\nimport { x402Client } from \"@x402/core/client\";\nimport { ExactEvmScheme, toClientEvmSigner } from \"@x402/evm\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { createWalletClient, http } from \"viem\";\nimport { baseSepolia } from \"viem/chains\";\n\nconst account = privateKeyToAccount(\"0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\");\nconst wallet = createWalletClient({ account, chain: baseSepolia, transport: http(\"https://sepolia.base.org\") });\nconst client = new x402Client().register(\"eip155:84532\", new ExactEvmScheme(toClientEvmSigner(wallet)));\nconst f = wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch, client);\nconst r = await f(\"http://localhost:4022/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.405\");\nconsole.log(\"status\", r.status);\nconsole.log(\"PAYMENT-RESPONSE:\", r.headers.get(\"PAYMENT-RESPONSE\"));\nconsole.log((await r.text()).slice(0, 600));\nEOF\ncp /tmp/smoke.mts $W/smoke.mts; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvx6g1MefAXLVbcprrz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JWLK8gmkkPtF97NYYMDTax","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"W=/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvyFh22to98Y9Ppo5ed","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0164hpUXsXj5Kw3wMEGM3g2Y","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"W=/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace; cd $W && cat > verify-sig.mts <<'EOF'\nimport { x402Client } from \"@x402/core/client\";\nimport { ExactEvmScheme, toClientEvmSigner, authorizationTypes } from \"@x402/evm\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { createPublicClient, http, recoverTypedDataAddress, verifyTypedData } from \"viem\";\nimport { baseSepolia } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { decodePaymentRequiredHeader } from \"@x402/core/http\";\n\nconst account = privateKeyToAccount(\"0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\");\nconsole.log(\"client address\", account.address);\nconst publicClient = createPublicClient({ chain: baseSepolia, transport: http(\"https://sepolia.base.org\") });\nconst client = new x402Client().register(\"eip155:84532\", new ExactEvmScheme(toClientEvmSigner(account, publicClient)));\n\nconst r402 = await fetch(\"http://localhost:47021/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.405\");\nconst required = decodePaymentRequiredHeader(r402.headers.get(\"PAYMENT-REQUIRED\")!);\nconst reqs = required.accepts[0];\nconst payload: any = await (client as any).createPayment(2, reqs);\nconst auth = (payload.payload ?? payload).authorization ?? payload.authorization;\nconst sig = (payload.payload ?? payload).signature ?? payload.signature;\nconsole.log(\"authorization\", auth);\n\nconst domain = { name: reqs.extra.name, version: reqs.extra.version, chainId: 84532, verifyingContract: reqs.asset };\nconst ok = await verifyTypedData({\n  address: account.address, domain, types: authorizationTypes as any,\n  primaryType: \"TransferWithAuthorization\", message: auth, signature: sig,\n});\nconsole.log(\"signature valid against the advertised domain:\", ok);\nconsole.log(\"recovered:\", await recoverTypedDataAddress({ domain, types: authorizationTypes as any, primaryType: \"TransferWithAuthorization\", message: auth, signature: sig }));\n\nconst bal = await publicClient.readContract({ address: reqs.asset as `0x${string}`, abi: [{type:\"function\",name:\"balanceOf\",inputs:[{type:\"address\"}],outputs:[{type:\"uint256\"}],stateMutability:\"view\"}], functionName: \"balanceOf\", args: [account.address] });\nconsole.log(\"client USDC balance on Base Sepolia:\", bal, \"(needs\", reqs.amount, \")\");\nEOF\nnpx tsx verify-sig.mts 2>&1 | tail -20","timeout":180000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1374,"cache_read_input_tokens":154114,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1374},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"170744db-e126-4996-ae6c-85a62c9b60ac","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:43:18.607Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvyELQVq9S6pKBG7iZm"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRw3PxiETdGqPAw8c3BZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0148DkErbTkRgevWt7aWn6cC","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"W=/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace; cd $W && cat > onchain-check.mts <<'EOF'\nimport { x402Client } from \"@x402/core/client\";\nimport { ExactEvmScheme, toClientEvmSigner } from \"@x402/evm\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { createPublicClient, http, hexToSignature } from \"viem\";\nimport { baseSepolia } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { decodePaymentRequiredHeader } from \"@x402/core/http\";\n\nconst account = privateKeyToAccount(\"0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\");\nconst pc = createPublicClient({ chain: baseSepolia, transport: http(\"https://sepolia.base.org\") });\nconst client = new x402Client().register(\"eip155:84532\", new ExactEvmScheme(toClientEvmSigner(account, pc)));\nconst r402 = await fetch(\"http://localhost:47021/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.405\");\nconst required = decodePaymentRequiredHeader(r402.headers.get(\"PAYMENT-REQUIRED\")!);\nconst reqs = required.accepts[0];\nconst p: any = await (client as any).createPaymentPayload(required, reqs);\nconst { authorization: a, signature } = p.payload;\n\nconst ABI = [{ type: \"function\", name: \"transferWithAuthorization\", stateMutability: \"nonpayable\",\n  inputs: [{name:\"from\",type:\"address\"},{name:\"to\",type:\"address\"},{name:\"value\",type:\"uint256\"},\n    {name:\"validAfter\",type:\"uint256\"},{name:\"validBefore\",type:\"uint256\"},{name:\"nonce\",type:\"bytes32\"},\n    {name:\"signature\",type:\"bytes\"}], outputs: [] }] as const;\ntry {\n  await pc.simulateContract({ address: reqs.asset as `0x${string}`, abi: ABI, functionName: \"transferWithAuthorization\",\n    args: [a.from, a.to, BigInt(a.value), BigInt(a.validAfter), BigInt(a.validBefore), a.nonce, signature],\n    account: \"0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dEaD\" });\n  console.log(\"USDC ACCEPTS the authorization — signature + domain are correct on-chain.\");\n} catch (e: any) { console.log(\"USDC REJECTS:\", (e.shortMessage ?? e.message).split(\"\\n\")[0]); }\nEOF\nnpx tsx onchain-check.mts 2>&1 | tail -8","timeout":180000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1920,"cache_read_input_tokens":158966,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1920},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"dfc7e4b0-b786-49e2-a20b-5272c2a6a4f5","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:44:25.920Z","request_id":"req_011CeRw3Nvx9dFui2ADBTrfV"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRwGkZoxcwLNRb4e9KKC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014bZtAWC8pKRXfNfNHkQsnA","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat >> /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192628Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/design.md <<'MARKDOWN'\n\n---\n\n## Appendix — what was verified, and how\n\nNothing above is quoted from memory of a spec. Every ERC-8004 signature was recovered by\nscanning the deployed implementation bytecode for `PUSH4` selectors and matching them\nagainst candidates, then confirmed by calling the live contracts on Base mainnet. Every\nx402 API was read from the installed `@x402/*` 2.23 type declarations, not from a\nremembered snippet.\n\n**Checked against Base mainnet:**\n\n- Both registries have code at the mainnet addresses, and both are ERC-1967 proxies\n  (Identity impl `0x7274e874…`, Reputation impl `0x16e0fa7f…`).\n- `giveFeedback` is `(uint256,int128,uint8,string,string,string,string,bytes32)` → `0x3c036a7e`.\n  `value` is **signed**; the tags are **`string`**.\n- `getSummary(uint256,address[],string,string)` → `0x81bbba58`, returning\n  `(uint64 count, int128 averageValue, uint8 averageValueDecimals)`. Confirmed by calling it\n  on a real agent (`count=15, averageValue=54, decimals=0`) and checking 54 against the mean\n  of that agent's 15 raw scores read via `readAllFeedback`.\n- An empty `clientAddresses` reverts with **`clientAddresses required`** — the registry\n  itself refuses to produce an unfiltered average.\n- `giveFeedback` simulated from `ownerOf(agentId)` reverts with **`Self-feedback not\n  allowed`**; the identical call from an unrelated address succeeds. The registry does *not*\n  require the rater to have paid — which is exactly why §4.3's client filtering matters.\n- Event topics `Registered(uint256,string,address)` and `NewFeedback(...)` taken from live\n  logs; the `NewFeedback` layout was confirmed by decoding a real emission (`tag1` appears\n  both hashed in topic 3 and plain in the data).\n- The `registration-v1` JSON shape in §2.1 mirrors a registration file actually served\n  through `tokenURI` on Base today.\n\n**Checked against the installed SDK:**\n\n- `paymentMiddleware(routes, resourceServer)` — routes **plus** a resource server. The\n  one-argument `paymentMiddleware(config)` shape is v1 and does not exist here. Route keys\n  are `\"GET /forecast\"` (verb-prefixed patterns).\n- Headers are `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` / `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` / `PAYMENT-RESPONSE`.\n- `@x402/evm`'s asset table for `eip155:8453` is\n  `{0x833589fC…2913, \"USD Coin\", \"2\", 6, USDC}` with no `assetTransferMethod` override →\n  the EIP-3009 path.\n- `parsePrice` passes an explicit `AssetAmount` through untouched, so `extra.name`/`extra.version`\n  are the integrator's job. `server.ts` therefore derives them from `findDefaultAsset(asset,\n  network)` rather than hardcoding, so the EIP-712 domain can never drift from `USDC_ADDRESS`.\n- On the client side `wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch, x402Client)` takes an **x402 client**, not a\n  bare account; `x402Fetch`/`createWallet` do not exist in the scoped packages.\n\n**Exercised live:**\n\n- `GET /forecast` with no payment returns `402` with a well-formed `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header\n  (`amount: \"350000\"`, correct asset, `payTo`, `extra`) plus the identity/trust JSON body.\n- The free discovery endpoints return correct documents; the bazaar extension passes the\n  SDK's own schema validation.\n- `GET /reputation?clients=…` performs a real `getSummary` against the ReputationRegistry on\n  Base mainnet and returns the same `count=15, average=54` computed independently above; the\n  unfiltered form is refused.\n- The `giveFeedback` calldata that `/receipts/:id` emits carries selector `0x3c036a7e`,\n  round-trips through `decodeFunctionData`, and encodes negative ratings correctly.\n\n**Not exercised, and why.** The signed-payment leg (`PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` → facilitator\n`verify`/`settle` → `200`) was not completed end to end. Mainnet settlement needs CDP\ncredentials, which are not present here; and the sandbox's Base Sepolia substitute is not a\nfaithful chain — it rejects an EIP-3009 signature that provably recovers to the payer under\na domain whose separator I computed by hand and matched bit-for-bit against the token's own\n`DOMAIN_SEPARATOR()`. So that leg is verified by construction (correct requirements on the\nwire, correct EIP-712 domain, SDK-typed wiring) but not by a settled transaction. Run it\nagainst Base mainnet with CDP keys before trusting it with real USDC.\nMARKDOWN\necho ok"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1338,"cache_read_input_tokens":175216,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1338},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"a4402e23-95c0-42a7-b429-11e85d0c7be0","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:47:21.005Z","request_id":"req_011CeRwGjeFUF7XHFNAXQy2G"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FDoSEAAFBzL1PTkCNdDGcc","type":"tool_result","content":"const ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\n\n/** CAIP-2. Base mainnet. The ERC-8004 pair below is the *mainnet* pair — see design.md §5. */\nconst NETWORK = (process.env.NETWORK ?? \"eip155:8453\") as Network;\n\n/**\n * Native USDC on Base. Not 0xA0b86991…eB48 (Ethereum mainnet USDC) and not\n * 0xd9aAEc86…b6CA (bridged USDbC) — both are the wrong contract for a Base route.\n */\nconst USDC = (process.env.USDC_ADDRESS ??\n  \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/** $0.35 in token base units. USDC has 6 decimals, so 0.35 → 350000. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_LABEL = \"$0.35\";\n\n/**\n * EIP-712 domain of the token being paid in. The client signs the EIP-3009\n * `transferWithAuthorization` against this, so a wrong `name`/`version` means every\n * signature is rejected. Resolved from the SDK's asset table for the configured\n * (asset, network) so it can never drift from USDC_ADDRESS; override only for a token\n * the SDK does not know. For native USDC on Base this is (\"USD Coin\", \"2\").\n */\nconst assetDefaults = findDefaultAsset(USDC, NETWORK);\nconst USDC_EIP712 = {\n  name: process.env.USDC_EIP712_NAME ?? assetDefaults?.name,\n  version: process.env.USDC_EIP712_VERSION ?? assetDefaults?.version,\n};\nif (!USDC_EIP712.name || !USDC_EIP712.version) {\n  throw new Error(\n    `No EIP-712 domain known for asset ${USDC} on ${NETWORK}. ` +\n      \"Set USDC_EIP712_NAME and USDC_EIP712_VERSION — without them the client signs \" +\n      \"transferWithAuthorization against the wrong domain separator and no payment verifies.\",\n  );\n}\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = (process.env.IDENTITY_REGISTRY ??\n  \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\") as `0x${string}`;\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = (process.env.REPUTATION_REGISTRY ??\n  \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/** USDC recipient. Needs no ETH — the facilitator pays settlement gas. */\nconst PAY_TO = (process.env.PAY_TO ??\n  \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/**\n * The ERC-721 tokenId the IdentityRegistry assigned us at register(). Not a UUID,\n * slug or hash we chose. Unset until step 5 of the checklist in design.md §6.\n */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ? BigInt(process.env.AGENT_ID) : null;","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"60bcbe66-aebb-49a4-957f-079b2a1893c7","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:47:25.913Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"const ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\n\n/** CAIP-2. Base mainnet. The ERC-8004 pair below is the *mainnet* pair — see design.md §5. */\nconst NETWORK = (process.env.NETWORK ?? \"eip155:8453\") as Network;\n\n/**\n * Native USDC on Base. Not 0xA0b86991…eB48 (Ethereum mainnet USDC) and not\n * 0xd9aAEc86…b6CA (bridged USDbC) — both are the wrong contract for a Base route.\n */\nconst USDC = (process.env.USDC_ADDRESS ??\n  \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/** $0.35 in token base units. USDC has 6 decimals, so 0.35 → 350000. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_LABEL = \"$0.35\";\n\n/**\n * EIP-712 domain of the token being paid in. The client signs the EIP-3009\n * `transferWithAuthorization` against this, so a wrong `name`/`version` means every\n * signature is rejected. Resolved from the SDK's asset table for the configured\n * (asset, network) so it can never drift from USDC_ADDRESS; override only for a token\n * the SDK does not know. For native USDC on Base this is (\"USD Coin\", \"2\").\n */\nconst assetDefaults = findDefaultAsset(USDC, NETWORK);\nconst USDC_EIP712 = {\n  name: process.env.USDC_EIP712_NAME ?? assetDefaults?.name,\n  version: process.env.USDC_EIP712_VERSION ?? assetDefaults?.version,\n};\nif (!USDC_EIP712.name || !USDC_EIP712.version) {\n  throw new Error(\n    `No EIP-712 domain known for asset ${USDC} on ${NETWORK}. ` +\n      \"Set USDC_EIP712_NAME and USDC_EIP712_VERSION — without them the client signs \" +\n      \"transferWithAuthorization against the wrong domain separator and no payment verifies.\",\n  );\n}\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = (process.env.IDENTITY_REGISTRY ??\n  \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\") as `0x${string}`;\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = (process.env.REPUTATION_REGISTRY ??\n  \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/** USDC recipient. Needs no ETH — the facilitator pays settlement gas. */\nconst PAY_TO = (process.env.PAY_TO ??\n  \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") as `0x${string}`;\n\n/**\n * The ERC-721 tokenId the IdentityRegistry assigned us at register(). Not a UUID,\n * slug or hash we chose. Unset until step 5 of the checklist in design.md §6.\n */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ? BigInt(process.env.AGENT_ID) : null;","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRwK2rQLCMhVpSwN4ZH1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `design.md` and `server.ts` are in the workspace, typechecking clean, with a working `npm start`.\n\n## What carries each constraint\n\n| Constraint | Answer |\n|---|---|\n| Stranger discovers + judges you, no human, no catalog you own | **ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry** on Base `0x8004A169…`. `register(agentURI)` mints an ERC-721 — **the agentId is the tokenId the registry assigns**, not a slug you pick |\n| Per call, no accounts/keys/invoices | **x402**: `402` + `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` → sign → retry with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` → `200` + `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` |\n| Caller has USDC, no ETH | **EIP-3009** — the client signs `transferWithAuthorization` offchain, the facilitator submits and pays gas. Not 4337, not an unnamed relayer, not `permit`+`transferFrom` |\n| Post-call rating others can act on | **ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry** `giveFeedback(...)`, client-attested — you cannot write it yourself |\n\n## Things worth flagging\n\n**Domain binding.** The agentURI *is* `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json`, so the domain serving the paid endpoints is the domain serving the registration file — that is what binds them, and design.md §2.2 says so explicitly rather than leaving it implied. The server keeps serving that path regardless, so moving the agentURI to IPFS later doesn't break the binding. The A2A `agent-card.json` is served too, but as a capability descriptor only; it binds nothing.\n\n**The EIP-712 domain is derived, not hardcoded.** `parsePrice` passes an explicit `AssetAmount` through untouched, so `extra: {name, version}` is the integrator's job — get it wrong and every signature fails. `server.ts` resolves it via `findDefaultAsset(asset, network)` so it can't drift from `USDC_ADDRESS`. Amount on the wire is `\"350000\"`, and the asset is native Base USDC `0x833589fC…2913`.\n\n**Sybil.** `getSummary` *reverts* on an empty client set (`clientAddresses required`) — the registry refuses to compute an unfiltered average. `/reputation` mirrors that refusal and labels itself non-authoritative.\n\n**One gap I couldn't close for the caller, documented in §4.4:** paying needs no ETH, but `giveFeedback` is a real Base transaction and we can't pay its gas — a sender who is us is rejected. EIP-7702 is the right answer there specifically because the client's *address survives*, and that address is what `getSummary` filters on; a fresh ERC-4337 account would orphan its payment history from its rating history.\n\n## Verification\n\nI read the deployed bytecode rather than trusting recall: `giveFeedback` is `(uint256,int128,uint8,string,string,string,string,bytes32)` — `value` is **signed**, tags are **`string`**, not `bytes32`. `getSummary`'s return shape was confirmed by decoding a live call and checking the average against the raw scores. `Self-feedback not allowed` was confirmed by simulating the call from the agent's owner. `/reputation` was exercised against real Base mainnet.\n\n**Not verified:** the signed-payment leg never completed. Mainnet settlement needs CDP credentials that aren't present, and the sandbox's Base Sepolia is not a faithful chain — it rejects an EIP-3009 signature that provably recovers to the payer under a domain whose separator I hand-computed and matched against the token's own `DOMAIN_SEPARATOR()`. So the 402 and the requirements on the wire are verified; the settle path is verified by construction only. Run it on mainnet with CDP keys before pointing real USDC at it."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1310,"cache_read_input_tokens":178476,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1310},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c2bdef34-720b-4f8f-aa8a-32122b5885db","uuid":"14b14d33-2668-4d00-8db9-242aaf4e3766","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:47:43.405Z","request_id":"req_011CeRwK1qPPRvhKsgoTqJQt"}
